Overview / venue

Wynn Las Vegas Poker

3131 S Las Vegas Blvd, Las Vegas, NV 89109 · Wynn Las Vegas

coverage: Daily recurring schedule + WPT World Championship series PDFs (fetched; the daily sheets state re-entry/add-on terms explicitly). events listed: 117 confirmed single-entry: 0 hotels in range: 2

Daily recurring schedule + WPT World Championship series PDFs (fetched; the daily sheets state re-entry/add-on terms explicitly).

Wynn's own daily sheets publish re-entry and add-on allowances, which disqualifies those events from a strict single-entry list — but it means their entry rules are confirmed rather than unknown.

Single entry

nomultiple entries allowedretrieved 2026-11-17

Unlimited re-entry for each starting flight will be available until the start of level 9. A player who advances to Day 2 is prohibited from entering a subsequent starting flight. A player that has advanced to Day 2 who enters a starting flight in error will be disqualified from the subsequent flight and will not be entitled to a refund of their Tournament re-entry fee. The player will be permitted to play Day 2 with their Tournament chip stack from the original starting flight.

A player who advances to Day 2 is prohibited from entering a subsequent starting flight. A player that has advanced to Day 2 who enters a starting flight in error willretrieved 2026-11-17

One (1) Entry and One (1) re-entry for each starting flight will be available until the start of level 10 (approximately 6:45 p.m.). A player who advances to Day 2 is prohibited from entering a subsequent starting flight. A player that has advanced to Day 2 who enters a starting flight in error will be disqualified from the subsequent flight and will not be entitled to a refund of their Tournament re-entry fee. The player will be permitted to play Day 2 with their Tournament chip stack from the original starting flight.

The player will be permitted to play Day 2 with their Tournament chip stack from the original starting flight.

Unlimited re-entry for each starting flight will be available until the start of level 10. A player who advances to Day 2 is prohibited from entering a subsequent starting flight. A player that has advanced to Day 2 who enters a starting flight in error will be disqualified from the subsequent flight and will not be entitled to a refund of their Tournament re-entry fee. The player will be permitted to play Day 2 with their Tournament chip stack from the original starting flight.

Flagged for this venue

  • F3 No walking minutes for any hotel↔venue pair — No official property page publishes a door-to-door walking time to another property's poker room, and this environment has no Google/Apple Maps access. The ≤30-minute filter therefore reports 'unverified' rather than passing or failing any hotel.
  • F15 Room tax 13.38% and Venetian/Palazzo parking $20/$40 are not restated by today's capture — The official Venetian terms page was captured with the sentence 'Taxes, Surcharges, and Resort Fee Daily rates do not include the applicable Clark County Room Tax of 13' — it breaks at the decimal point, because the fact extractor stops a sentence at any period. The calculator therefore still applies 13.38%, and the parking amounts are kept, but neither is marked verified. The extractor pattern was fixed to allow a period inside a number, so the next scheduled capture either confirms both figures verbatim or the values are removed.
  • F16 Walking times are measured distances at an assumed pace, not official minutes — method: OpenStreetMap foot-route length between each property's building point and each event room's building point, converted to minutes at a stated pace; pace: 80.0 m per minute; why not the server durations: the demo router's foot durations came back at 4-8 m/s, which is not walking speed, so only its route lengths were used; cross-checks: 12 pairs were re-routed one at a time and agreed to within 0.05 m; captures: data/raw/f0d7de66-geocode.txt, data/raw/f0d7de66-osrm.txt. No Strip property publishes a door-to-door walking time to another property's poker room, so these are the closest thing to a measured figure that exists, and every cell carries official:false. The points are building centroids, so an interior connection (Venetian-Palazzo, Wynn-Encore) is not represented by the street route.
